### 2026-07-31 07:31:35  #其他

【人形機器人現“電量焦慮” 行業正在積極探索換電等不同路線】2到4個小時，是當前全球主流量產人形機器人的普遍續航時長。這意味著，一臺號稱能替代工廠工人的機器人，撐不滿一個標準班次。當人形機器人從實驗室走向工廠乃至家庭，續航這道坎，正在成為規模化落地最現實的“攔路虎”。在近日舉辦的世界人工智慧大會（WAIC2026）上，均勝電子旗下均恩新能源總經理徐偉向記者點出了行業面臨的六大挑戰：續航時間短、有效載荷受限、內部空間緊張、補能時間過長、安全風險突出以及高動態運動下的瞬時大功率需求。“但這些可以歸結為兩個核心矛盾——續航能力不足和安全挑戰。”徐偉直言。人形機器人電池續航問題，需要電池材料、電機效率、控制演算法、電源管理等多個環節的協同突破，而非單一技術一站式解決。目前，行業正在積極探索換電、固態電池、半固態電池等不同路線。換電是短期最務實的方案，固態電池被寄予厚望但距離大規模量產仍有距離，半固態電池則成為當前最可行的過渡路線。

### 2026-07-31 07:31:29  #市場 #觀點

【中信建投：今年轉債投資難度明顯提升，核心在於正股偏弱與估值偏高並存】中信建投研報指出，今年轉債投資難度明顯提升，核心在於正股偏弱與估值偏高並存。年初以來轉債對應正股等權指數下跌約3.7%，權益端形成負向貢獻；但轉債指數仍保持小幅正收益，主要依賴溢價率擴張和中低價品種抗跌。7月以來轉債抗跌特徵突出。創業板指、科創50等成長指數回撤較深，但中證轉債和轉債等權指數調整幅度有限；分層看，高價轉債仍受正股拖累，中低價轉債更具韌性，反映資金更偏向債底保護、票息收益和下修預期。抗跌支撐主要來自固收+配置需求與轉債供給收縮的錯配，而非新債納入貢獻。若後續正股持續走弱，偏高溢價率可能面臨重估壓力，風險或沿“轉股價值下行-溢價率壓縮-信用風險擔憂”路徑傳導。

### 2026-07-31 07:28:51  #其他

【人民日報聚焦：上海建設6G未來產業培育區，探索新一代通訊網應用場景】走進位於上海市松江區泗涇鎮的上海6G未來產業培育區核心承載區——6G信通智谷，戴上眼鏡，鏡身的電極片接觸皮膚，即能接收腦電波，感知狀態與情緒變化。“根據情緒切歌、點咖啡都可以實現。”開發者介紹，不僅如此，在阿爾茨海默病初露跡象時，眼鏡能及時發出提醒；即便在沒有地面訊號的深山與荒漠，藏在鏡腿裡的模組也能直連天上的衛星，確保裝置正常執行。“這是未來新一代通訊網提供普惠智慧服務的一個直觀場景。”上海6G信通智谷董事長金軍介紹，6G不僅意味著速度快、覆蓋廣，更關鍵的是推動“萬物智聯”。如果說5G連線了人與人、人與物，6G則將實現人、物與智慧體之間的深度互動。經過多年發展，我國建成全球規模最大、技術領先的資訊通訊網路。6G技術研發與標準制定全面推進，第一階段6G技術試驗順利完成，萬兆光網已開始試點部署應用，400G超高速光傳輸系統、新型超低損耗光纖光纜實現規模部署。衛星網際網路建設快速推進，空間網路密集組網。工業網際網路、低空智聯網等融合性新型基礎設施有序推進並取得積極進展。（人民日報）

Brent Heads for Strong Monthly Advance — Brent traded near $89 per barrel on Friday and was on track to post a monthly gain of almost 25% as renewed fighting between the US and Iran threatened further disruptions to Middle Eastern energy supplies. The US military launched fresh strikes on Iranian targets in response to Tehran’s attacks on US assets across the region, dimming hopes for any near-term diplomatic breakthrough. Still, tanker traffic through the Strait of Hormuz increased after a recent slowdown, allowing millions of barrels of crude to pass. Meanwhile, in the Red Sea, Saudi Arabia held talks with representatives from 43 countries about forming a maritime coalition to safeguard shipping routes following the blockade imposed by Iran-backed Houthi militants last week. Elsewhere, traders also remained wary of supply disruptions in the Black Sea after loadings at the export terminal critical to Kazakhstan’s crude shipments were suspended once again this week following fresh attacks on oil tankers.

### 2026-07-31 07:26:29  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution

【ANTHROPIC discloses three security-test intrusions by AI models】ANTHROPIC said on Thursday some of its most capable models accessed real systems without authorization during pre-deployment cybersecurity testing. After OpenAI disclosed a testing-period intrusion into Hugging Face infrastructure, ANTHROPIC reviewed more than 141,000 security assessments and identified three intrusion incidents. A miscommunication with testing partner Irregular left an evaluation environment inadvertently connected to the internet, allowing OPUS 4.7, MYTHOS 5 and an internal research model to reach systems at three organisations during capture-the-flag exercises. The incidents date back to April; ANTHROPIC has notified the three affected parties, two of which had not previously detected the activity. Unlike OpenAI’s case, ANTHROPIC says its models did not exploit zero-day vulnerabilities; access resulted from an environment configuration error. ANTHROPIC also did not deploy extra safeguards during evaluation to measure baseline model capability. The disclosures underscore that frontier AI models can touch real-world systems during security tests, raising questions about how labs secure evaluation environments.

【信維通訊：公司目前商業衛星通訊相關業務產品的生產、交付及研發工作均處於正常狀態】信維通訊公告，近日，據日經亞洲（Nikkei Asia）引述訊息人士稱“某北美企業據報要求供應商排除中國籍員工及裝置”，大量投資者對該事件表達了關切。針對上述情況，公司董事會高度重視，立即組織相關部門對公司涉及商業衛星通訊業務的供應鏈、生產佈局及人員配置情況進行了全面核查。經核實，該訊息缺乏事實依據，公司與客戶的商務合作條款均未發生任何變化。公司目前商業衛星通訊相關業務產品的生產、交付及研發工作均處於正常狀態。公司與相關大客戶保持著長期、穩定的合作關係，各項業務正按計劃穩步推進。公司已於去年將商業衛星通訊相關產品的生產線轉移至越南等海外生產基地。目前，公司海外基地的生產設施完善、技術團隊成熟，相關產線已實現規模化生產並持續向客戶穩定交付。

### 2026-07-31 07:23:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Korea #Retail Sales MoM #Importance 1

South Korea Retail Sales Growth at 5-Month High — Retail sales in South Korea rose by 2.7% month-over-month in June 2026, accelerating from a 0.1% rise in the previous month and marking the strongest growth since January. The increase was largely driven by higher sales of durable goods, particularly automobiles. On a year-on-year basis, retail sales increased by 4.2% in June, the fastest rise in three months and up from a downwardly revised 1.5% gain in the preceding period. As a key gauge of private consumption, the latest figures suggest household spending gained solid momentum at the end of the second quarter, providing a positive signal for domestic demand.

【碳達峰硬性指標落地 汽車行業存量調整開啟攻堅期】國務院近期印發的《“十五五”碳達峰行動方案》指出，“十五五”時期是實現碳達峰的關鍵期、攻堅期。《方案》設定交通領域低碳轉型目標，明確到2030年，新能源汽車保有量佔比力爭達到30%,新能源營運交通工具保有量佔比達到25%。目前國內新能源汽車增量端滲透率已逼近五成，但存量市場轉型進度明顯滯後。根據公安部的資料，截至今年6月底，全國機動車保有量達4.76億輛，其中汽車3.71億輛。而新能源汽車保有量4897萬輛，佔汽車總量的13.19%，距離30%的目標還有近17個百分點的差距。業內分析認為，“十五五”時期，汽車產業減碳重心將迎來根本性切換，即由過去側重拉動新能源新車增量，轉向增量擴容與存量燃油車更新置換雙線並行，公共領域、貨運重卡等高頻高排放營運車輛成為存量替代攻堅主戰場。依託財政專項補貼、多元動力技術迭代、全域補能網路配套，汽車行業將承擔交通領域降碳核心重任，為全國如期完成碳達峰任務築牢產業底盤。（中國證券報）

【摩根大通：日本進一步實施匯市干預的空間或將有限】摩根大通策略師Junya Tanase表示，日本當局選擇在日本央行7月31日政策決議前出手干預匯市，是預判植田和男行長釋放偏鴿論調或將再度引發日元拋售。現階段很難就加息節奏釋放明確訊號，且市場已基本消化10月加息預期，“本次政策溝通存在被市場解讀為鴿派傾向的風險。”倘若當局確實實施干預，此次干預規模大機率可觀，累計干預金額或已突破2024年15萬億日元（摺合940億美元）的規模。“若外匯儲備進一步下降，依靠儲備管理手段恐難以恢復至原有水平，意味著後續追加干預的空間有限。”近期官方表態提及動用政府養老金投資基金，以遏制日元走弱、抑制日本國債收益率上行。這表明隨著匯市干預空間持續收窄，政策制定者已開始考量替代政策工具。

【傳統資產ETF走強 機構看好消費賽道投資機會】7月30日，傳統資產迎來爆發，油氣、白酒、銀行、消費、紅利等防禦風格較強的ETF有較好表現。具體來看，基本面ETF建信當日漲幅最高，達4.58%，標普油氣ETF富國、酒ETF鵬華、銀行ETF天弘等漲超3%。國聯安基金表示，本輪消費復甦與紅利低波板塊的走強，由內外雙重邏輯共振驅動。外部層面，全球資本市場波動加劇，海外主要股指及韓國股指震盪走弱。美聯儲利率決議落地後，內部分歧持續存在，市場普遍預期高利率環境持續，高估值成長資產估值承壓，倒逼資金主動降低組合波動，轉向穩健防禦品種。內部層面，消費板塊此前歷經長時間深度調整，機構持倉籌碼充分出清，交易擁擠度處於低位水平。紅利低波板塊估值迴歸合理區間，憑藉穩定的現金流回報與可持續的股息率，在震盪市中具備突出的避險優勢。（中國證券報）

South Korea Industrial Output Growth Hits 6-Year High — South Korea’s industrial production grew 6.4% month-on-month in June 2026, rebounding from a downwardly revised 2.9% decline in the previous month, which was the steepest drop in seven months. It was the first increase in industrial production in three months and the strongest growth since June 2020, mainly boosted by a sharp rebound in manufacturing (6.8% vs -3.2% in May). Meanwhile, services activity rose at a slower pace of 0.7%, following a 1.0% increase in the previous month. New Zealand Stocks Head for Monthly Gain — New Zealand's stocks climbed 92 points, or 0.7%, to 13,854 in Friday morning trade after retreating in the previous session, mainly buoyed by financials, communication services, and real estate. The broader index rebounded from a more than one-week low touched a day earlier, tracking a rally on Wall Street overnight amid a rebound in technology stocks. Fresh data also lifted sentiment, as New Zealand's consumer confidence hit a five-month high. Moderating oil prices also fueled sentiment, easing inflation concerns and tempering expectations of an interest rate hike. However, traders awaited the release of China's PMI data due later today and trade data due next week. Caution also prevailed ahead of the release of New Zealand's unemployment data due next week. Among the early gainers were Infratil (2.1%), Meridian Energy (1.8%), Fisher & Paykel (1.2%), and Ebos Group (1.0%). For the week, the index is heading for a 0.6% gain, while posting a 1.7% increase for the month.

【US senators urge Apple to avoid chips from two Chinese memory firms; researcher warns of supply-chain damage】Bloomberg reported July 30 that several US senators sent a joint letter to Apple CEO Tim Cook urging the company not to use storage chips from ChangXin Memory Technologies (CXMT) and Yangtze Memory Technologies (YMTC), even for products sold only in China. Apple has been seeking to source storage chips from Chinese suppliers amid capacity shortages, tight supply and rising prices. ### 2026-07-31 06:41:15  #國際

【特朗普稱與哈馬斯及加沙其他武裝團體達成解除武裝協議】當地時間7月30日，美國總統特朗普表示，“和平委員會”當天達成一項“歷史性協議”，哈馬斯及加沙地帶其他所有武裝團體將全面解除武裝。
特朗普稱，這項協議是落實其“加沙20點計劃”的重要里程碑。根據協議，加沙地帶將逐步由新的巴勒斯坦政府接管，該政府將與“和平委員會”合作，負責當地治理。加沙今後不得再被用作發動襲擊的基地，以色列也將獲得其所需的安全保障。
協議將分階段實施。隨著解除武裝程序完成，以色列軍隊將逐步撤出；國際穩定部隊將與新組建的巴勒斯坦警察部隊合作，承擔維護加沙及周邊地區安全的責任。
特朗普同時感謝埃及、卡達和土耳其為推動協議所作的斡旋，並稱儘管已取得重大進展，後續仍有大量工作需要完成。（央視新聞）

【蘋果第三季度財報五大要點總結】
1、蘋果盤後股價大跌超 6%。投資者擔憂兩大問題：大中華區市場需求走弱、服務業務營收顯著低於預期。蘋果將業績壓力歸因於匯率逆風，以及應用商店商業模式遭遇監管調整帶來的衝擊。
2、候任 CEO 約翰・特努斯被問及行業競爭形勢，但基本回避正面回應，僅表示公司正在推進多款新產品研發。
3、即將卸任的 CEO 蒂姆・庫克稱，蘋果正在評估各類方案緩解記憶體供應緊張問題，但預判相關成本仍會持續上漲。公司預計第四財季營收增速介於 9%–11%，低於市場一致預期 12%。
4、庫克發表任職 CEO 的收官感言，向投資者與分析師致謝。有分析師提到：庫克剛接任執行長時，蘋果全年營收規模，僅等同於蘋果當下一個季度的營收。
5、Mac、iPad、iPhone 目前面臨嚴重供應瓶頸，短缺狀況還將延續。庫克素來被譽為供應鏈管理大師，這場收官財報電話會議整體表現不及預期。

【今年以來公募獲配定增超過380億元，依賴折價獲利的空間正在壓縮】公募排排網資料顯示，按增發上市日統計，截至7月28日，今年以來共有24家公募機構參與75家公司股票定增，合計獲配金額380.05億元，較2025年同期的139億元增長173.42%。按7月28日收盤價計算，參與定增的公募機構目前平均浮盈比例達到15.9%，其中超過半數的機構獲得正收益。雖然整體收益情況較好，但今年以來公募機構定增收益已經出現明顯分化。公募排排網資料顯示，在上述75單專案中，公募在26單上實現浮盈，其中12單浮盈比例超過10%。部分科技製造領域專案表現突出，部分專案收益表現相對有限。業內人士表示，定增收益分化反映了市場環境的變化。過去，定增投資的重要收益來源之一是發行折價帶來的安全墊，但隨著市場參與者增多、優質專案競爭加劇，折價空間正在逐步收窄。（上證報）

### 2026-07-31 05:11:32  #MKT News #Important

【Trump administration weighs $100,000 fee for international graduates seeking Optional Practical Training (OPT)】Persons familiar told media the Trump administration is considering a $100,000 fee for international graduates who seek to remain in the United States under Optional Practical Training (OPT). OPT allows international university graduates to work in the US for one to three years on a student visa; the Institute of International Education reported about 300,000 students were on OPT last fall, roughly 25% of foreign students in the US. If enacted, the fee would likely curtail demand for US study and materially affect US universities, Silicon Valley hiring and Wall Street talent pipelines. The proposal mirrors an earlier administration objective to impose a $100,000 charge on H‑1B visas. The Department of Homeland Security said the policy is under internal discussion, is not final, and it is unclear whether it has White House approval or whether the fee would be paid by students or employers.

Other notable gainers included Petrobras (+2%), Axia (+3.6%), and WEG (+2.6%). ### 2026-07-31 05:07:31  #公司 #市場

【蘋果大中華和服務業務季度銷售遜於預期 iPhone表現依然穩健】蘋果上財季服務業務和大中華區銷售增長均慢於分析師預期，引發投資者對這兩個關鍵市場的擔憂。儘管總營收高於分析師預測，但蘋果第三財季大中華銷售額為188億美元，明顯低於分析師預計的196億美元。服務業務收入為307億美元，也不及314億美元的預測。蘋果一直受到儲存晶片和電腦處理器短缺的困擾，這迫使公司上月上調了Mac和iPad價格。供應緊張導致Mac mini和Mac Studio等重要電腦產品的交貨等待期延長。某種程度上，上財季也是執行長蒂姆·庫克的謝幕之作。庫克將於9月1日把管理權交給硬體業務負責人約翰·特努斯。自2011年執掌蘋果以來，庫克推動了產品線多元化，並將公司年度銷售額提高到接近5000億美元。蘋果今年以來累計上漲23%，表現超過許多科技同業。作為蘋果最大的收入來源，iPhone是週四業績中的亮點。該產品季度營收增長22%，至543億美元，高於536億美元的市場預期。相關資料表明，蘋果去年9月推出的iPhone 17系列需求仍然穩健。該公司今年3月還推出了新的低價機型iPhone 17e。包括Apple Music、App Store、iCloud訂閱、流媒體影片及其他數字服務在內的服務業務收入上財季增長12%。截至6月27日的季度，蘋果每股收益升至2.02美元，高於分析師平均預期的1.89美元。

【Rivian汽車業務營收增23% R2 SUV開啟交付】Rivian第二季度營收中，汽車業務收入11.4億美元，軟體及服務收入5.15億美元。該業績略高於公司上月在公告擬公開發行7500萬股A類普通股時提前披露的第二季度營收預期區間15.5億至16.5億美元。公司稱，汽車業務營收同比增長23%，主要源於車輛交付量提升14%，以及合規積分相關收入增加1.03億美元。 Rivian第二季度歸屬於普通股股東的淨虧損為8.37億美元，摺合每股虧損0.63美元，相較於2025年第二季度減虧2.78億美元，每股虧損收窄0.34美元。 Rivian此前表示，上調交付指引是因為第二季度電動配送貨車及旗艦R1系列產品交付量有所提升。 公司本季度還啟動了中型R2 SUV的交付工作，正在其位於伊利諾伊州諾莫爾市的唯一生產工廠推進該車型產能爬坡，這座工廠年產能可達16萬輛。 Rivian執行長RJ·斯卡林吉週四在接受採訪時表示：“R2現已交到客戶手中，我對此倍感振奮，市場對這款產品的整體反饋和反響都非常出色。當然，這也是我們邁向盈利道路上的重要一步。”

【Apple Q3 revenue slightly above estimates; Greater China lags, shares down 4% after-hours】Apple reported third fiscal quarter revenue of $109.42 bln, up 16.4% YoY and marginally above the $108.65 bln consensus. EPS was $2.02, including $0.11 from a tariff rebate; adjusted EPS exceeded the $1.89 estimate. iPhone sales rose 21.7% to $54.25 bln, beating the $53.86 bln forecast and setting a Q3 record. Global memory-chip shortages lifted Mac and iPad prices this quarter, which the company says prompted earlier iPhone purchases; Apple has not raised iPhone list prices but the Street generally expects price increases at the September launch. Greater China revenue was $18.82 bln versus $19.67 bln expected. Apple shares fell about 4% in after-hours trading.

【UEFA and 55 members vote to boycott all FIFA competitions】On July 30, UEFA and its 55 member associations voted to boycott all FIFA competitions unless FIFA withdraws proposals to transfer ownership and commercial rights of the World Cup and other FIFA tournaments to private investors and provides binding guarantees to keep governance and events out of private capital. UEFA said it rejects FIFA president Gianni Infantino’s plan to form a $20bn subsidiary to run the World Cup and other tournaments and sell minority stakes to private investors. CONCACAF and its 41 member associations also rejected the proposal to sell World Cup equity but did not announce a boycott.

US Stocks Rebound as Chipmakers Rally — US stock indices closed higher on Thursday, rebounding from the previous session's losses as chipmakers rallied and investors assessed earnings. The S&P 500 gained 1.7%, the Nasdaq jumped 3.4%, and the Dow added 614 points. Microsoft surged 15.5% after reporting 43% cloud revenue growth, its strongest since 2022, while keeping capital expenditure guidance broadly in line with expectations. Chipmakers jumped after yesterday's selloff. Micron rose 18.4%, AMD gained 13%, Intel added 11.3%, and Sandisk soared 26% as investors bought the dip on expectations the AI-driven rally still has room to run. Oracle jumped 8.3% after expanding its Google Gemini AI partnership. Those gains outweighed a disappointing forecast from Meta (-7.9%). Treasury yields remained high despite soft Q2 GDP growth. After holding rates yesterday, Fed Chair Warsh's reluctance to rule out further tightening reinforced concerns that rates could stay high. Apple (-1.4%) and Amazon (+3.9%) report later.

Brent Near $89 — Brent fell nearly 1.8% to below $89 a barrel on Thursday, following a 7.9% surge in the previous session, amid signs of improving shipping activity through the Strait of Hormuz and continued diplomatic efforts to ease regional tensions, despite ongoing hostilities in the Middle East. According to Kpler, 14 commodity vessels transited the Strait of Hormuz in both directions on Wednesday, up from single-digit daily crossings recorded last week. Qatar also dispatched its first LNG cargo through the waterway, while reports indicated that Saudi Arabia had proposed a naval coalition to safeguard key trade routes. Meanwhile, elevated crude inventories in China continued to weigh on import demand, further easing fears of a near-term supply squeeze. However, geopolitical risks remained elevated after US forces struck dozens of Iranian military targets in an effort to weaken Tehran's ability to threaten regional shipping and US allies.

【白宮稱哈馬斯或數日內同意解除武裝協議】據報道，協議被視為落實特朗普“20點和平計劃”的關鍵一步。核心內容包括：哈馬斯解除輕重武器、移交地道網路和武器設施資訊，並在6至8個月內完成去軍事化；隨後由新的“加沙管理國家委員會”接管治理，統一政府、法律和安全體系。訊息人士稱，美方、埃及、卡達和土耳其近幾個月持續向哈馬斯施壓，試圖切斷其資金來源，並利用其經濟困境推動其接受協議。談判目前在埃及阿拉曼舉行，並進入最後階段，但哈馬斯尚未正式批准。據悉，埃及情報總局局長哈桑·拉沙德在談判中發揮關鍵作用，並與哈馬斯新領導人哈利勒·哈亞保持聯絡。與此同時，伊朗方面則敦促哈馬斯不要急於簽署協議，爭取更多時間。根據協議草案，哈馬斯成員可獲得特赦並留在加沙；武裝人員上繳武器可獲得補償；以色列將在協議落實後逐步撤軍，並停止針對哈馬斯人員的定點清除行動。不過，以色列方面仍對哈馬斯是否真正願意解除武裝持懷疑態度，認為其可能只是緩解壓力的策略。特朗普政府則認為，如果協議最終達成，將為結束加沙戰爭、啟動重建程序邁出重要一步。

【White House says Hamas could agree to disarmament accord within days】Axios, citing four people familiar with the matter, reports a draft accord viewed as key to implementing Trump’s 20-point peace plan and that the White House says Hamas could agree within days. Core terms would require Hamas to surrender light and heavy weapons, hand over tunnel networks and weapons-facility intelligence, and complete demilitarization within 6–8 months. A new Gaza National Administration Committee would assume governance and unify government, legal and security systems. The US, Egypt, Qatar and Turkey have pressured Hamas in recent months, including efforts to cut funding and exploit economic strain to force acceptance. Talks are reportedly in their final stage in Egypt’s El Alamein, but Hamas has not formally approved. Egyptian intelligence chief Hassan Rashad is said to be playing a key role and liaising with new Hamas leader Khalil al-Hayya; Iran is urging Hamas to delay. Under the draft, Hamas members could receive amnesty and remain in Gaza; militants who surrender weapons could receive compensation; Israel would withdraw gradually after implementation and halt targeted strikes on Hamas personnel. Israel remains skeptical Hamas will fully disarm. The Trump administration says a finalized accord would be a major step toward ending the Gaza war and launching reconstruction.

In the Middle East, oil-shuttling services picked up with data suggesting more barrels are leaving the Persian Gulf through vessels with transponders off, dampening concerns over energy supply. In the Bab el-Mandeb strait, two Saudi tankers also managed to cross the waterway by going dark. Meanwhile, EIA data showed US gasoline inventories rose slightly last week, defying expectations for a decline though stocks remained 6% below the five-year seasonal average. Geopolitical risks nevertheless persisted despite continued mediation efforts by China and Oman to facilitate US-Iran talks. ### 2026-07-31 01:11:01  #公司 #國際

【應對成本競爭壓力 OpenAI降價至多80%鞏固市場】OpenAI 於週四宣佈下調兩款最新人工智慧模型 GPT-5.6 Terra 與 GPT-5.6 Luna 的定價，距離兩款模型公開發布僅約三週。企業客戶對成本愈發敏感，若無法清晰看到投資回報，就不願部署高價模型，OpenAI 正面臨滿足這類客戶需求的壓力。同時公司還需要抵禦谷歌、微軟等科技巨頭的競爭，這些對手一直在主推高價效比模型。該公司週四表示，Terra 價格下調 20%，輸入代幣費率降至每百萬代幣 2 美元，輸出代幣費率為每百萬代幣 12 美元；Luna 降價 80%，輸入代幣費率降至每百萬代幣 0.2 美元，輸出代幣費率為每百萬代幣 1.2 美元。Sol 定價維持不變。OpenAI 在公告中稱：“我們的核心戰略仍是同步提升模型能力與執行效率，力求每一代智慧模型都能以更低成本完成更多工。”2022 年 ChatGPT 問世掀起人工智慧熱潮，推動美國各行各業爭相落地這項技術，並鼓勵員工廣泛使用，催生了所謂 “最大化呼叫代幣” 階段。彼時企業鼓勵員工儘可能使用人工智慧，無需顧慮成本。但隨著人工智慧相關開支飆升，部分企業相關成本達到數十億美元規模，眾多公司開始主動控制支出。

### 2026-07-31 00:48:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Currency #Importance 1

Japanese Yen Rebounds on Likely Intervention — The Japanese yen surged past 160 per dollar on Thursday from the 40-year low of 164 in the prior session amid the likely intervention by the Ministry of Finance. The probable move followed the $74.2 billion in selling by the MoF on the month to May 27th. This brought relief to recent pressure from high energy prices, fiscal concerns, and wide interest rate differentials. Soaring LNG and petrol prices due to the war in Iran raised dollar bids in Japan by energy importers and pressured growth due to lower margins, pressuring the domestic currency. In the meantime, fiscal concerns were magnified by the domestic tax panel reiterating the proposal to cut food taxes to 1% from the current 8%, adding to the supply of yen through fresh debt. Lastly, the Bank of Japan is due to hold rates unchanged tomorrow following their hike in June. The BoJ is expected to still deliver hikes this year, but the expectation of hikes by the US Fed limited any narrowing in the rate differential.

### 2026-07-31 00:19:52  #國際

【報道：特朗普政府考慮向希望留美工作的國際學生收取 10 萬美元費用】知情人士透露，特朗普政府正醞釀一項政策：向希望在美國高校畢業後留美工作的國際學生收取 10 萬美元費用。此舉將大幅削弱美國留學對海外學子的吸引力。如若落地，這項收費政策將有力推進特朗普總統限制各類合法移民的施政目標。受衝擊最大的將是美國高校（國際學生是學校穩定的收入來源），以及矽谷、華爾街頭部企業 —— 這類企業大量聘用國際畢業生填補技術崗位缺口。這筆費用將針對選擇性實習訓練（OPT）專案收取。OPT 允許持學生簽證的國際畢業生在美國工作 1 至 3 年。可查最新資料顯示，2024 年約有 41.9 萬名外籍人士依靠 OPT 在美國就業。本質上，該政策旨在實現政府此前對外籍專業人士 H-1B 簽證徵收 10 萬美元費用的目標。去年這項簽證收費方案曾在矽谷引發巨大恐慌，但波士頓一家上訴法院上週叫停了政府徵收 H-1B 相關費用的計劃。

### 2026-07-31 00:09:39  #公司 #國際

【國際足聯擬拆分賽事商業權益募資 200 億美元，歐足聯威脅全面抵制世界盃】週二，總部位於瑞士的非營利機構國際足聯（FIFA）公佈一項重磅方案：計劃將旗下頂級賽事的商業權益剝離，成立一傢俬營公司，並向投資者出讓約 20% 股份，該企業估值達 200 億美元。但僅僅到週四，反對聲浪洶湧。歐洲足球管理機構召開緊急會議後，多個歐洲國家宣佈抵制包括世界盃在內的所有國際足聯賽事。歐足聯（UEFA）在會後表態：“這套模式不該出現在世界足壇。足球的未來，不能由首要目標是最大化收益的資本訴求所左右…… 基於今日討論結果，只要上述方案尚未撤銷，所有歐足聯旗下國家隊將不再參加任何國際足聯賽事。”國際足聯辯稱，這是最高效的募資方式，籌集的資金將分配給 211 個會員協會，並承諾未來四年向每家協會額外提供至多 4000 萬美元資金。對於國際足聯主席詹尼・因凡蒂諾而言，此舉也是進一步拉近與特朗普陣營聯絡的契機。擬定的投資方牽頭方為約書亞・庫什納旗下的興盛資本（Thrive Capital）。約書亞・庫什納是賈裡德・庫什納的弟弟，而賈裡德・庫什納是特朗普的女婿、前總統高階顧問。

【霍爾木茲海峽石油轉運貿易在短暫低迷後回升】近期一度放緩的霍爾木茲海峽原油接駁運輸服務重新活躍，幫助數百萬桶原油運出海峽。在中東地區敵對局勢升級之際，這種運輸方式再次發揮關鍵作用。這種轉運模式興起於衝突最激烈的時期，成為部分產油國維持出口的重要生命線。相關船隻將原油從波斯灣運出——通常會關閉船舶自動識別系統（AIS）應答器以避免被發現——隨後在霍爾木茲海峽外與另一艘油輪進行船對船（STS）轉運，再由後者將原油運往全球各地買家。儘管目前經霍爾木茲海峽運輸的原油仍低於戰前水平，但已成功運出的原油在緩解市場對油價飆升的擔憂方面發揮了重要作用。兩位直接瞭解情況的人士表示，至少有兩家參與霍爾木茲海峽運輸的航運企業，目前轉運量已接近敵對局勢升級前的水平。

【廣東清遠通報“長湖水庫出現死魚”：水位突降所致，水質正常】7月30日晚，廣東清遠市生態環境局釋出情況通報：近日，我局關注到網民反映英德市滃江長湖水庫出現死魚，擔心水體汙染。我局第一時間聯合廣東省清遠生態環境監測站開展現場取樣監測。自7月30日上午8時開始，在英德市長湖水庫上下游、滃江干支流、北江干流沿線布點，連續開展4輪次全面水質監測。監測結果顯示，水質正常，未檢出有毒有害因子。經核查，為應對臺風“紅霞”帶來的強降雨影響，長湖水庫提前騰庫容，導致水庫水位突降，網箱魚類缺氧，出現死魚現象。水庫水位恢復後，未再出現死魚現象。下一步，我局將繼續密切監測流域水質情況，確保水環境安全。

### 2026-07-30 23:39:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Falls After Thursday Rally — Brent crude oil traded around $90 per barrel on Thursday after surging 7.9% in the previous session, as markets balanced renewed geopolitical tensions against signs that oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz were improving. According to Kpler, 14 commodity vessels transited Hormuz in both directions on Wednesday, up from single-digit levels seen last week. Meanwhile, ample crude inventories in China continued to limit import demand, easing some supply concerns. Still, US forces struck dozens of Iranian military targets in an effort to curb Tehran’s ability to threaten regional shipping and US allies. Also, the East-West pipeline to bypass Hormuz that Saudi Arabia has increasingly relied on now faces risks from Iran-backed Houthis. On top of that, the Caspian Pipeline Consortium suspended oil loadings at its Black Sea terminal after two tankers associated with the facility were attacked overnight.

【美軍：近期伊朗發起的襲擊行動未造成任何美軍飛機損毀】美國中央司令部：過去數小時內，伊朗官方媒體持續轉載伊朗伊斯蘭革命衛隊的不實說法，主要有三點。

第一點說法：伊朗伊斯蘭革命衛隊再度聲稱，霍爾木茲海峽開放通航通道對商船構成安全威脅。
事實：真正威脅商船及其船員安全的，是伊朗伊斯蘭革命衛隊發出的口頭威脅與襲擊企圖。

第二點說法：伊朗伊斯蘭革命衛隊宣稱，在近期伊朗襲擊美軍基地行動中，摧毀了三架美軍F-35隱身戰機以及另外三架軍機。
事實：近期伊朗發起的襲擊行動未造成任何美軍飛機損毀。所有導彈與無人機均被攔截，或是未能抵達目標區域。

第三點說法：伊朗官方媒體稱油輪“諾拉號”突破了美國封鎖。
事實：這艘商船並未衝破美國構築的嚴密封鎖。二十餘艘美軍戰艦、數百架戰機以及數千名官兵保持戒備，持續全面執行封鎖任務。

Colombia Industrial Confidence Improves — Colombia’s industrial confidence indicator rose by 2.8 percentage points to -0.1% in June 2026, marking its strongest reading since February. 【Japan formally establishes National Intelligence Agency】On July 31 local time Japan formally established a National Intelligence Agency. The inaugural director is Hara Kazuya, who previously headed the National Police Agency’s Foreign Affairs Division, served as director general of the Security Bureau, and has been Cabinet intelligence officer since 2023. Under a law passed earlier by the Diet, Japan will build an intelligence architecture centered on a National Security Council with the National Intelligence Agency as the executive body. The agency will coordinate intelligence for national security and counterterrorism, conduct external intelligence activities related to foreign spies, and has authority to require ministries and agencies to provide intelligence.

【日本政府正式設立“國家情報局”】當地時間7月31日，日本政府正式設立“國家情報局”。首任局長為原和也。原和也曾歷任警察廳外事課課長、警備局局長等職務，自2023年起擔任內閣情報官。根據此前在日本國會透過的法案，日本將構建以“國家情報會議”為核心、“國家情報局”為執行機構的情報體系，負責統籌安全保障、反恐等領域的情報活動以及涉外國間諜的“對外情報活動”。“國家情報局”還被賦予要求政府各省廳向其提供情報的綜合協調權。在高市早苗政府強力推動下，日本國會眾議院4月23日透過一項法案，計劃設定“國家情報會議”和“國家情報局”，意圖在二戰後首次構建國家級情報統合體系。日本政府7月24日在內閣會議上決定，該機構首任局長選定內閣情報官原和也。（央視新聞）

【Analyst: Now a favorable window for Japanese authorities to support the yen】USD/JPY plunged as much as 3% to 158.34 on Thursday, off this week’s 40-year high and on course for its largest one-day fall since end-2022. Traders widely saw the move as bearing the hallmarks of Japanese government FX intervention after months of yen weakness. Tokyo has warned it would act if the yen kept sliding; surging energy import costs have amplified household pressure and raised the political cost of depreciation. The move came ahead of Friday’s BOJ policy meeting and after the Fed left rates unchanged in a split vote on Wednesday, which weakened the dollar. FX strategists said month‑end position-squaring, weak US data and dollar softness created a favorable window for authorities to buy yen. Japan’s finance minister Katayama has repeatedly reiterated the government stands ready to act in FX markets.

【G30連霍高速新疆“咽喉”段完成改擴建通車運營】30日，G30連霍高速公路星星峽至哈密段、哈密至吐峪溝段完成改擴建，全線通車運營，進出新疆“咽喉”路段由過去雙向四車道拓寬為雙向八車道，進出疆高速通行能力翻倍。 G30連霍高速是國家高速公路網中重要的東西橫向主幹線，也是進出新疆高速公路三大通道之一。星哈段、哈吐段分別位於新疆哈密市、吐魯番市境內，是進出新疆重要的“咽喉”路段。該路段投用多年，隨著交通量逐年攀升，高峰時段通行不暢，日漸成為制約區域發展的“瓶頸”。 2023年，星哈段、哈吐段改擴建工程啟動，專案總里程為537公里，設計速度為120公里/小時。 為進一步提升通行效率，新疆取消了星哈段、哈吐段主線收費站，幫助車輛快速透過，同時配套建設防風設施與大風預警系統，提升出行的安全性與舒適度。 新疆維吾爾自治區交通運輸廳介紹，截至目前，新疆擁有G7京新高速、G30連霍高速、依吞布拉克至若羌高速三條進出疆高速公路通道，所有地州市實現了高速公路全覆蓋，高速公路通車裡程已突破8000公里。（新華社）

【“維港紐帶創科基金”在港簽約 首批專案聚焦硬科技】“維港紐帶創科基金”簽約儀式30日在港舉行，首批9個滬港科創儲備專案正式亮相。這是香港特區政府“創科創投基金”最佳化計劃下特區政府與內地機構合作簽約設立的首隻基金。“維港紐帶創科基金”總規模6億港元，由上海國有資本投資有限公司作為基石投資人，中信資本擔任管理人，交銀國際、太平資管（香港）擔任共同管理人，香港特區政府“創科創投基金”以有限合夥人身份參與出資。香港特區政府於2017年成立20億港元“創科創投基金”，旨在鼓勵更多風險投資（風投）基金共同投資於本地創新及科技（創科）初創企業。2024年施政報告提出最佳化“創科創投基金”，調撥15億港元與業界以配對形式成立聯合基金，投資策略性產業的初創企業。簽約儀式上，香港特區政府創新科技及工業局局長孫東致辭表示，“創科創投基金”最佳化計劃採用政府與市場1:3配資模式，推出以來收到超60份基金申請。他認為，“創科創投基金”最佳化計劃是有為政府與高效市場有機結合，撬動耐心資本培育新質生產力；扶持三大策略性產業初創企業加快商業化；與實力資本聯手助力初創企業“走出去”，藉助網路幫助港企開拓市場。（新華社）

【染料原料單日飆漲64%、均價增逾三成，行業景氣度持續上行】染料原料H酸均價單日增逾三成，行業開啟新一輪漲價週期。據百川盈孚資料，7月28日，活性染料關鍵原料H酸行業均價從6.1萬元/噸上漲31.15%至8萬元/噸。當日，H酸頭部廠商大柴旦和信科技有限公司出廠價上調至10萬元/噸，單日漲幅達64%，較年初4萬元/噸的價格累計上漲近150%，觸及歷史高位。活性染料與分散染料是染料行業兩大核心品類，各自對應不同的關鍵中間體。作為活性染料的關鍵中間體，H酸生產涉及磺化等高危工藝，並伴隨大量廢酸處理，環保與安全監管門檻極高，近年來中小型落後產能持續退出。據染料龍頭閏土股份（002440）披露的投資者關係活動記錄表，目前我國H酸有效產能已不足6萬噸，存在10%以上供給缺口。（澎湃）

【Meta未來資料中心租賃承諾激增至2790億美元，全力押注AI算力基建】7月29日，美國科技巨頭Meta Platforms Inc.（META）在最新監管檔案中披露，為支撐人工智慧（AI）領域的快速擴張，公司未來的資料中心租賃承諾規模已大幅攀升。截至今年6月30日，Meta尚未開始履行的租賃承諾總額達到2790億美元，較上一季度的1830億美元大幅增長53%，顯示出其在AI基礎設施領域的投資步伐正顯著加快。Meta在檔案中指出，這些尚未反映在資產負債表中的未來支出，主要涉及資料中心、託管機房以及特定網路基礎設施的建設與運營。僅在今年7月，Meta就新增了高達680億美元的租賃承諾，這些合同預計將在2027年和2028年陸續開始執行。這一鉅額租賃承諾的背後，是Meta為搶佔AI賽道而進行的“軍備競賽”。隨著AI模型訓練和推理對算力需求的爆發式增長，全球科技巨頭正以前所未有的規模鎖定資料中心資源。資料顯示，包括Meta、微軟、亞馬遜和甲骨文在內的頭部企業，未來需要履行的資料中心租賃承諾總額目前已突破8500億美元。

### 2026-07-30 22:42:16  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natgas Prices Edge Up Slightly After EIA Data — US natural gas prices edged higher to around $2.75 per MMBtu after the latest EIA report showed a smaller-than-expected increase in storage last week. Energy firms added 28 billion cubic feet of gas in the week ended July 24, below market expectations for a 35 bcf build. Despite the modest rebound, prices remained near their lowest level since early May as inventories stayed 6.4% above the five-year seasonal average, indicating ample supply. Production also remained strong, with output in the Lower 48 states averaging 110.6 bcfd so far in July, matching the record monthly high set in December 2025. LNG export demand softened slightly, with flows to major export terminals averaging 17.2 bcfd, down from 17.4 bcfd in June, partly because of scheduled maintenance at Freeport LNG’s Texas facility. Updated weather forecasts calling for cooler temperatures across the central and eastern US in the coming weeks also weighed on prices by reducing expected air-conditioning demand.

### 2026-07-30 22:40:50  #資料 #市場 #央行 #國際

【美國二季度經濟增速放緩至1.5%，核心通脹指標出現降溫跡象】當地時間7月30日，美國經濟分析局公佈初步估算資料顯示，美國第二季度實際國內生產總值（GDP）按年率增長1.5%，低於第一季度的2.1%，也低於市場預期的1.8%。儘管整體經濟增速有所放緩，但作為經濟主要驅動力的居民消費和企業投資表現強勁，顯示出美國內需依然具備較強韌性。資料顯示，佔美國經濟活動約三分之二的個人消費支出在第二季度按年率增長3.2%，較第一季度0.5%的增速顯著回升。6月份經通脹調整後的實際消費支出環比增長0.4%，創下近一年來的最高月度增速。季末汽油價格回落、年度退稅紅利釋放以及商家暑期促銷活動，共同提振了家庭消費意願，有效對沖了地緣衝突推高物價的負面衝擊。除消費外，企業商業投資的強勢增長成為經濟增長的重要增量。在人工智慧產業熱潮的帶動下，Meta、微軟等頭部科技企業持續加碼AI賽道，大規模佈局資料中心建設並擴充算力資本開支。即便在高利率環境下，AI領域的企業資本支出熱度依舊不減，彰顯市場對AI產業長期發展的堅定信心。此外，衡量內需真實動能的“國內私人最終銷售額”在第二季度大漲3.9%，創2023年初以來最高水平，進一步印證了美國潛在需求的強勁。在通脹方面，同日公佈的配套資料顯示，美聯儲最關注的通脹指標——個人消費支出（PCE）物價指數6月環比下降0.1%，為2020年新冠疫情以來首次出現月度負增長，同比漲幅由前月的4.1%收窄至3.7%。剔除波動較大的食品和能源後，核心PCE物價指數環比僅上漲0.1%，低於市場預期的0.2%，同比增速從3.4%小幅回落至3.3%。分析指出，本輪通脹短期降溫的核心驅動因素為能源價格下行。受美伊達成臨時停火協議影響，國際油價階段性回落，直接拖累整體PCE指數走低。然而，此次通脹回落具備較強偶發性，美伊雙方核心分歧尚未化解，臨時停火協議穩定性薄弱，國際油價依舊處於歷史相對高位，為後續通脹反彈埋下隱患。作為美聯儲錨定通脹趨勢的核心指標，當前核心PCE同比3.3%的增速仍遠高於美聯儲2%的長期目標，且已連續第六年維持目標值上方，表明美國通脹的結構性內生壓力並未徹底消退。

【UK short-dated gilts surge; market sharply cuts September rate-hike odds】Short-dated UK gilts rallied after the Bank of England left rates unchanged and said domestic inflationary pressures are easing. The two-year gilt yield fell 11 bps to 4.34%, the largest one-day drop since June 12. Governor Bailey said he did not want attendees to leave the press conference with the impression the committee is progressively moving toward tightening; gilts extended gains after the remark. Money markets now price about 32 bps of cumulative BoE hikes by year-end, down from roughly 38 bps before the decision, and the probability of a September hike fell from near 60% to below 40%. Aberdeen Asset Management portfolio manager James Lynch said the threshold for a BoE hike is very high and he expects rates to remain on hold for the rest of the year.

Canadian Natural (-0.5%) and Fairfax Financial (-1%) are due to report after the market close.

### 2026-07-30 22:07:38  #MKT News #Market Regions #Japan & APAC #USDJPY #Impact bearish #DXY

【Yen surges 2% amid speculation of renewed Japanese FX intervention】The yen rallied about 2% as USD/JPY fell roughly 2.6% intraday, the largest move since Japan’s earlier intervention; USD/JPY has plunged over 300 pips to 159.19 at time of writing. Markets are pricing an increased chance of fresh Japanese authorities support for the yen. The currency had earlier weakened to near 40-year lows. Japan carried out record FX intervention totaling JPY 11.73 tln (~$73.2 bln) between Apr 28 and May 27 to prevent USD/JPY from falling below the 160 level, but the yen remained under pressure thereafter. Japan’s Ministry of Finance foreign reserve data indicate funds for its recent interventions likely came from sales of foreign securities holdings, including US Treasuries.

### 2026-07-30 21:57:45  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 1

Dollar at 6-Week Low — The dollar index extended its losses to around 100.3 on Thursday, its lowest level in about six weeks, after falling 0.6% in the previous session. Investors continued to assess the latest FOMC decision and Fed Chair Warsh's press conference. As expected, the Fed left the federal funds rate unchanged, although three FOMC members voted in favor of a rate hike. While Chair Warsh reiterated the Fed's commitment to bringing inflation under control and stressed that policymakers would not hesitate to act if necessary, he was not among those advocating an immediate increase and refrained from providing clear forward guidance. As a result, investors further scaled back expectations for an imminent rate hike and increasingly viewed the central bank as merely postponing what they see as an inevitable increase in borrowing costs. The implied probability of a 25-basis-point hike in September has fallen to around 55%, down from nearly 80% before the Fed's decision.

Silver Rises Above $58 on Weaker Dollar — Silver climbed above $58 per ounce on Thursday, supported by a weaker US dollar as investors assessed the Federal Reserve's decision to keep interest rates unchanged and monitored escalating tensions in the Middle East. The Fed left its benchmark rate at 3.50%-3.75%, while Chair Kevin Warsh reiterated the central bank's commitment to bringing inflation under control. Meanwhile, the US military said it struck dozens of IRGC targets in Iran after Tehran launched ballistic missiles at US troops in Jordan, heightening concerns that a broader conflict could reignite inflationary pressures. Markets are pricing in roughly a 60% chance of a Fed rate hike in September. Higher interest rates typically weigh on non-yielding precious metals such as silver by increasing the opportunity cost of holding them.

Gold Extends Gains on Softer Dollar — Gold climbed to $4,080 per ounce on Thursday, extending Wednesday's 2% rally as a weaker US dollar provided support, while investors assessed the Federal Reserve's decision to leave rates unchanged and monitored escalating tensions in the Middle East. The Fed kept rates at 3.50%-3.75%, with Chair Kevin Warsh reaffirming the central bank's commitment to bringing inflation down. Meanwhile, the US military said it struck dozens of IRGC targets in Iran after Tehran launched ballistic missiles at US troops in Jordan, raising concerns over renewed inflationary pressures. Although gold is widely viewed as a hedge against inflation and geopolitical uncertainty, expectations of higher interest rates for longer tend to weigh on the non-yielding metal. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 60% chance of a Fed rate hike in September. Separately, the World Gold Council said unofficial gold inflows into India have risen since the country increased import tariffs earlier this year.

【漁翁資訊欺詐發行領千萬罰單，保薦機構是西部證券】近日，江西證監局公示行政處罰決定書，漁翁資訊科技股份有限公司（下稱“漁翁資訊”）科創板IPO申報材料存在重大財務造假、刻意隱瞞股權代持關鍵資訊等違法違規行為，企業及相關責任主體合計被罰1790萬元。值得關注的是，漁翁資訊早在兩年前便主動撤回IPO申請，稽核流程終止，但依舊遭到監管重罰。處罰決定書顯示，上交所於2022年12月受理漁翁資訊科創板上市申請。經過多輪監管問詢，2024年6月，漁翁資訊聯合保薦機構遞交撤回申請，上交所隨即終止本次IPO稽核。該公司上市計劃擬募集資金3.34億元，保薦機構是西部證券，專案保薦代表人系高峰、蘇華峰。對此，有律師認為，漁翁資訊欺詐發行行為涉及財務造假、招股書存虛假記載，保薦機構恐難逃追責。（紅星資本局）

The S&P 500 and the Dow gained over 0.5%, while the Nasdaq 100 gained 1.5%. Microsoft surged nearly 12% after posting a 43% growth in cloud revenues, the most since 2022. Also, chip producers enjoyed some respite following their selloff yesterday with Micron and Sandisk rising over 9%. Conversely, Meta tanked 10% after its guidance missed expectations, while markets remained skeptical on whether its AI labs will drive revenue as signaled by the hyperscaler. ### 2026-07-30 21:25:55  #市場 #央行 #國際

【美聯儲9月若維持利率不變，或引發美債收益率大幅飆升 】分析師愛德華·哈里森（Edward Harrison）撰文指出，儘管當前市場定價顯示美聯儲在9月份加息的機率接近三分之二，但美聯儲仍有可能再次出人意料地選擇按兵不動。若這一情形發生，將進一步推高美國國債收益率曲線，引發市場劇烈動盪。文章分析稱，美聯儲主席凱文·沃什（Kevin Warsh）在7月議息會議後的新聞釋出會上曾暗示，近期金融市場條件的自發收緊，在一定程度上替代了美聯儲在7月採取行動的需要。基於這一邏輯，市場很可能在9月面臨同樣的博弈局面。資料顯示，儘管6月份核心個人消費支出（PCE）物價指數低於預期，但30年期美國國債實際收益率在週四仍一度飆升至2.98%。此外，即將公佈的經濟資料也可能為美聯儲提供觀望的理由。一方面，核心PCE數值低於市場預期；另一方面，美國經濟分析局（BEA）正在重新計算物價上漲的統計方式，批評人士認為該方式存在缺陷，其調整結果可能會在賬面上降低通脹壓力。

### 2026-07-30 21:19:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #30 Year Bond Yield #Importance 1

US 30-Year Bond Yield Surges to 19-Year High — The yield on the 30-year US Treasury bond surged to above 5.2%, the highest since 2007, to reflect concerns of higher inflation in the longer term. The Federal Reserve held rates unchanged in its July meeting, aligned with the consensus, despite rate futures showing that one third of the market was positioned for a hike. The rise for the 30-year tenure led the movements among Treasuries following controversial statements by Fed Chairman Warsh during the press conference. The reluctance to signal a rate hike as the Fed's preferred response to inflation drove long-dated yields to surge, even though those on shorter maturities fell. The Chair also signaled a positive response in seeing long-term yields rise in Q2, akin to a policy tool, despite signaling the contrary in his speech during the ECB central bank forum one month prior. Underlying inflation gauges in the US flared higher in Q2 after tariffs and soaring energy prices lifted costs among various industries in the second quarter.

【據悉明星AI投資者在蒙受鉅額虧損後正在平倉】知情人士透露，由前OpenAI研究員Leopold Aschenbrenner創立、管理規模達240億美元的對沖基金Situational Awareness，在人工智慧概念股大幅下跌以及做空軟體股的押注失利後，正陸續平倉多項交易，並忙於籌措資金。知情人士表示，近幾周，Situational Awareness遭受重大損失。其持有的AI基礎設施投資（如SK海力士）股價下跌，而其針對Adobe等軟體公司的空頭頭寸則出現大幅虧損。目前尚無法確定該基金是透過協商出售資產來滿足追加保證金要求，還是已開始對整個投資組合進行更大範圍的清算。知情人士還表示，該基金還試圖透過出售未上市公司的股權來籌集流動性，其中包括其持有的Anthropic股份。目前尚不清楚該基金擬出售Anthropic股份的具體規模，不過熟悉交易流程的人士表示，已有潛在買家表示有意收購部分或全部股份。

### 2026-07-30 21:17:15  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #000660.KS #Impact bearish #ADBE.O #Impact bullish

【Report: $24bn hedge fund Situational Awareness liquidating positions after heavy losses】CNBC sources say Situational Awareness, a hedge fund founded by former OpenAI researcher Leopold Aschenbrenner and managing about $24bn, is unwinding trades after taking heavy losses in recent weeks. Losses followed a sell-off in AI-concept names and failed shorts on software firms: its AI-infrastructure holdings including SK Hynix fell, while short positions against Adobe incurred large losses. The fund is reportedly raising liquidity and has been selling assets, including stakes in private companies such as Anthropic; potential buyers have expressed interest in part or all of the Anthropic holding. Sources say it is unclear whether sales are negotiated to meet margin calls or part of a broader portfolio liquidation.

【可控核聚變產業智慧財產權運營中心在合肥啟動執行】7月30日，在安徽合肥召開的安徽省聚變產業聯合會第一屆會員代表大會第二次會議上，可控核聚變產業智慧財產權運營中心啟動執行，中心旨在聚焦聚變領域智慧財產權創造、保護與運用，打通成果轉化“最後一公里”。據悉，可控核聚變產業智慧財產權運營中心是國家智慧財產權局認定的首批產業智慧財產權運營中心之一，由安徽省市場監督管理局、中國科學院合肥物質科學研究院、安徽省聚變產業聯合會、聚變新能（安徽）有限公司共同啟動執行。未來，中心將透過構建專利池、促進產學研融合、提供專業智慧財產權服務等路徑，加速高價值專利落地轉化，打造聚變產業發展的加速器與價值轉化引擎。（新華社）

### 2026-07-30 21:09:02  #MKT News #Star Stocks #NVIDIA #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #TSM.N #Impact bullish #INTC.O #Impact bearish #NVDA.O #Impact mixed

【TSMC developing EMIB-like advanced packaging to rival Intel: The Information】The Information, citing two people familiar with the project, reports TSMC (TSM.N) is developing an advanced chip-packaging technology similar to Intel's (INTC.O) Embedded Multi-die Interconnect Bridge (EMIB). With AI driving demand to integrate more processors and high-bandwidth memory into larger multi-die packages, advanced packaging has emerged as a key industry bottleneck. Intel’s EMIB uses small silicon bridges to create high-speed links between processors and memory, enabling larger multi-chip AI designs and helping customers diversify supply chains; it has attracted interest from NVIDIA (NVDA.O). Sources say TSMC engineers internally call the work “EMIB-like” and have discussed the solution with some customers.

US Real Consumer Spending Holds Steady at 0.4% in June — Inflation-adjusted US personal spending rose 0.4% month-over-month in June 2026, matching May's pace. Goods spending accelerated to 0.7% from 0.6%, driven by stronger gains in motor vehicles and parts (2.2% vs. 1.9%), gasoline and other energy goods (0.7% vs. -3.3%), and clothing and footwear (1.3% vs. 0.9%). Meanwhile, services spending increased 0.3%, unchanged from May. Faster growth in recreation services (0.9% vs. 0.2%) and financial services and insurance (0.6% vs. 0.4%) offset slower gains in food services and accommodations (0.3% vs. 0.9%) and housing and utilities (0.0% vs. 0.2%). Spending on health care and transportation services remained steady at 0.3% each.

### 2026-07-30 20:58:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Wage Growth #Importance 1

Canada Weekly Earnings Growth Slows in May — Average weekly earnings of Canadian non-farm payroll employees rose 3.4% year-on-year to C$1,337.77 in May 2026, easing from a 3.8% increase in April and marking the slowest growth since February. The strongest wage gains were recorded in management of companies and enterprises (10%), utilities (9.2%), information and cultural industries (8.3%), retail trade (8.1%), and transportation and warehousing (7.1%). In contrast, earnings declined in real estate and rental and leasing (-1.8%) and construction (-0.1%). By province, Nunavut posted the largest increase (6.6%), followed by Nova Scotia (5.3%), Manitoba (4.8%), British Columbia (4.6%), and Prince Edward Island (4.5%), while Newfoundland and Labrador recorded a 1.3% decline. On a monthly basis, average weekly earnings fell 0.4%. Meanwhile, average weekly hours worked were unchanged at 33.4 hours.

【龔正會見美國霍尼韋爾科技董事長兼執行長柯偉茂】上海市市長龔正今天（7月30日）會見了美國霍尼韋爾科技董事長兼執行長柯偉茂一行。龔正說，當前，上海正在深入推進新型工業化、培育發展新質生產力，加快構建現代化產業體系，著力推動高質量發展。在這個過程中，需要世界知名企業積極參與，特別是高階製造、綠色低碳、城市更新等領域，都離不開相關的配套技術和服務支撐。希望霍尼韋爾科技持續加大在滬投資佈局，把更多核心業務、前沿技術和優質專案落在上海，助力產業轉型升級，深度融入本土製造供應鏈，推動上海產業智慧化、綠色化、融合化發展。同時，歡迎公司積極參加第九屆進博會，把更多新產品、新技術、新服務帶到上海、引入中國，共享中國和上海的發展機遇。我們將一如既往地打造市場化、法治化、國際化一流營商環境，持續最佳化為企服務，助力各類企業在滬實現更大發展。柯偉茂說，每一次來到上海，都被這座城市的活力感染。上海的發展方向與霍尼韋爾的業務發展方向高度契合，我們願在新興產業領域深化合作。此次霍尼韋爾中國增長峰會在上海舉辦，我們帶來了最新技術和解決方案。我們對中國和上海的發展充滿信心，將持續加大在滬投資，持續參與進博會，與上海開展更多合作。霍尼韋爾於今年6月拆分為3家獨立運營企業。霍尼韋爾科技是其中之一，公司在滬設立了中國區總部、研發中心等實體。 (澎湃新聞)

【U.S. initial jobless claims rise less than expected】U.S. initial jobless claims rose by 9,000 to a seasonally adjusted 197,000 in the week ending July 25, below economists' forecast of 200,000, the U.S. Labor Department said Thursday. The increase partly offset the prior week's fall to the lowest level since 1969; July claims are often volatile because automakers schedule annual plant shutdowns—this year GM kept most assembly lines running and Ford canceled traditional summer stoppages, which may have distorted seasonal-adjustment models. Economists said the U.S. labor market remains in a "slowing hires, slowing layoffs" state.

【寶馬上半年營業利潤同比下降近四成】德國汽車製造商寶馬集團30日公佈的2026年上半年財報顯示，受多重因素影響，集團上半年營業利潤同比下降近四成，預計全年汽車交付量將較上年下降。 財報顯示，今年上半年，寶馬集團營業利潤為36.4億歐元，同比下降37.4%；淨利潤為28.7億歐元，同比下降28.5%；營業收入為622.7億歐元，同比下降8%。此外，汽車交付量也降至115.7萬輛。 寶馬集團董事長米蘭·內德利科維奇表示，過去一年多來，多重因素疊加使公司經營環境更加嚴峻，包括高關稅、貿易壁壘和匯率波動帶來的不利影響，歐洲地區監管要求不斷提高，中東地區持續衝突以及全球市場競爭加劇等。財報顯示，當季汽車業務營業利潤率由去年同期5.4%降至2.3%。 內德利科維奇表示，汽車行業正經歷深刻變革，為應對當前挑戰、確保長期競爭力，公司正透過精簡組織架構等方式推進降本計劃。 寶馬日前宣佈，計劃自今年10月起至2027年年底前在全球削減約8000個工作崗位。預計其中超過一半的崗位在德國本土。據公司網站資料，截至2025年年底，該公司全球有員工約15.45萬人，其中德國員工超過8萬人。(新華社)

US Futures Hold Rebound — Futures tracking US equities held their rebounds on Thursday from sharp losses yesterday amid a batch of corporate earnings and respite for chip stocks. The S&P 500 and the Dow were over 0.5% higher, while the Nasdaq 100 gained 1.5%. Microsoft surged nearly 10% premarket after posting a 43% growth in cloud revenues, the most since 2022. ### 2026-07-30 20:44:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Personal Income #Importance 1

US Personal Income Rises Less Than Expected — US personal income rose 0.2% month-over-month in June 2026, falling short of market expectations for a 0.3% increase and slowing from a 0.7% gain in May. The increase was driven by higher employee compensation, income receipts on assets, and government social benefits, partly offset by a decline in farm proprietors' income. Employee compensation rose by $32.3 billion, led by a $19.6 billion increase in private-sector wages and salaries. Income from assets increased on higher dividend and interest earnings, while government social benefits were supported mainly by higher Medicare and social security benefits. The decline in farm proprietors' income reflected the timing of payments under the American Relief Act of 2025. Meanwhile, disposable personal income also rose 0.2%, slowing from 0.7% in May, while real disposable personal income increased 0.3%, accelerating from a 0.2% gain in the previous month.

【Fed-preferred PCE posts first monthly drop since 2020; risks remain】The Fed's preferred inflation gauge, the PCE price index, fell 0.1% MoM in June — the first monthly decline since 2020 — supporting the Fed's decision this week to hold rates unchanged. Headline PCE slowed to 3.7% YoY from 4.1%. Core PCE (excluding energy) rose 0.1% MoM, below expectations, and eased to 3.3% YoY from 3.4%. The June cooling was driven mainly by lower oil after a fragile US‑Iran temporary ceasefire opened space for peace talks. It remains unclear whether inflation will continue to retreat. The PCE gauge has stayed above the Fed's 2% target for the sixth consecutive year.

### 2026-07-30 20:42:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Personal Spending #Importance 1

US Consumer Spending Growth Slows in June — US personal spending increased by $65.2 billion, or 0.3%, in June 2026 from the previous month, slowing from an upwardly revised 0.9% gain in May, largely due to a sharp decline in gasoline spending as lower crude oil prices followed peace negotiations between the US and Iran. Goods spending rose $7.0 billion, as a $48.1 billion drop in spending on gasoline and other energy goods was partly offset by higher outlays on motor vehicles and parts (up $17.4 billion) and recreational goods and vehicles (up $13.8 billion). Meanwhile, services spending increased $58.2 billion, driven by health care (up $22.8 billion), financial services and insurance (up $14.0 billion), transportation services (up $7.1 billion), and recreation services (up $6.7 billion). Meanwhile, inflation-adjusted consumer spending advanced 0.4% in June, the same pace as in the previous period.

【小米澎程N90 Max後向全固態鐳射雷達由速騰聚創獨家供應】7月30日，小米汽車正式釋出SkyNomad小米澎程智慧可變大空間SUV。其中，大七座旗艦車型小米澎程N90 Max搭載由RoboSense速騰聚創獨家提供的後向全固態數字化鐳射雷達，成為小米汽車首款搭載後向固態鐳射雷達的量產車型。該雷達主要補充車尾近場及側後方三維感知資訊，可為車輛識別路沿、臺階、懸空及異形障礙物，以及側後方行人和車輛提供高精度點雲資料，並與攝像頭、超聲波雷達等感測器形成互補。速騰聚創基於自研晶片在全球率先實現車規級全固態鐳射雷達規模量產，此次搭載的後向鐳射雷達基於速騰聚創車規級E平臺打造。截至2026年4月，E平臺累計交付已突破30萬臺，重新整理全固態數字化鐳射雷達行業交付紀錄。

US Initial Jobless Claims Below Expectations — The number of people claiming unemployment benefits in the US were at 197,000 on the week ending July 25th, rebounding by 9,000 from the 57-year low in the previous period but still under median expectations of 200,000. The data continued to reflect a strong labor market in the US, aligned with statements from FOMC members that see the US economy in full employment. Meanwhile, initial claims filed by federal employees, which have been under scrutiny due the administration's efforts in decreasing the number of public workers, fell by 52 to 418.

US PCE Prices Fall as Expected — The US PCE price index decreased 0.1% month-over-month in June 2026, as expected, after a 0.5% rise in May. 【京東方A：玻璃基封裝載板試驗線已通線 鈣鈦礦中試線效率達20.11%】京東方A(000725.SZ)釋出投資者關係活動記錄表公告，公司玻璃基封裝載板試驗線於2026年上半年實現全自動化裝置通線，設計產能1000片/月。公司已完成大尺寸高層數(9-2-9，20層)玻璃基載板樣品開發和送樣，並透過多項信賴性標準測試。鈣鈦礦方面，公司採用剛性/柔性/疊層元件技術路線並行開發，手套箱效率記錄達27.94%，實驗線21.39%，中試線20.11%，柔性16.6%。公司計劃今年下半年在黑龍江漠河、新疆吐魯番和寧夏銀川開展極致條件實證測試。光互連方面，公司已成立MicroLED光互連繫統及玻璃載板CPO技術攻關專案組，進行前瞻技術預研。公司控股子公司合肥京東方顯示技術有限公司於近日完成少數股東定向減資，註冊資本由240億元減少至175.5億元，公司持股比例從36.67%提高至50.14%。未來隨著存量產線折舊持續減少，公司總體折舊金額將在2025年基礎上開始下降，資本開支金額也將逐漸降低。

US GDP Growth Slows in Q2 — The US economy expanded an annualized 1.5% in Q2 2026, below 2.1% in Q1 and forecasts of 2.1% the advance estimate from the BEA showed. The contributors were increases in consumer spending, investment, and exports that were partly offset by a decrease in government spending. Imports also went up. Compared to the first quarter, the deceleration in real GDP reflected a downturn in government spending and decelerations in investment and exports that were partly offset by an acceleration in consumer spending. Imports increased more in the second quarter than in the first quarter.

### 2026-07-30 20:27:45  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Stock Market #Importance 1

European Stocks Rebound — European stocks extended their gains on Thursday amid strong GDP data and favorable corporate results. The Euro STOXX 50 added nearly 1% to 6,300 and the STOXX Europe 600 gained 0.7% to 649. The Eurozone GDP expanded by 0.4% in the second quarter, firmly above market expectations of 0.2% to reflect some resilience to soaring energy prices and sovereign yields in the period. Still, economic optimism was buffered by elevated EU-harmonized inflation rates, with pickups across Spain and Germany. Schneider Electric surged 9% after raising its full-year targets. BBVA climbed 3.5% after reporting an 11.4% increase in net profit. Also, ING rose more than 4% after posing their results, lifting other banks. Other banks also rallied. On top of that, Ferrari maintained its slight gains after raising its guidance. On the downside, Adidas slumped more than 16% after its heavy marketing campaigns around the FIFA World Cup triggered a surge in outlays.

【受益世界盃及跑步業務，阿迪達斯上調全年業績指引】7月30日，阿迪達斯釋出2026年Q2財報。第二季度，阿迪達斯全球營收達67億歐元，在貨幣中性下（下同），同比增長14%，再創歷史新高；營業利潤達5.74億歐元，同比增長5%；毛利率同比提升0.8個百分點至52.5%。阿迪達斯全球CEO古爾登指出，本季度除歐洲外所有地區均實現兩位數增長。作為阿迪達斯最為重要的戰略市場之一，大中華區業績連續十三個季度實現高質量的增長。財報顯示，2026年第二季度，阿迪達斯大中華區實現營收9.5億歐元，同比增長15%。介面新聞記者瞭解到，在足球和跑步專案的帶動下，運動表現業務增長39%。生活方式業務營收增長2%。其中，跑步業務實現近30%的增長。得益於強勁表現，阿迪達斯上調2026全年業績指引，全年營收增幅區間為9%-10%，此前預期為高個位數增長。

### 2026-07-30 20:25:00  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #GBPUSD #Impact bullish

【Bank of England Governor Bailey: Second‑round inflation cannot be ruled out; may raise rates further if needed】After the policy decision, Governor Bailey said the Bank of England stands ready to adjust policy as the outlook changes. There is currently little sign that recent energy-price rises have become embedded and no evidence yet of second‑round inflation, but the risk cannot be ruled out and the BoE will remain vigilant. If the conflict persists and boosts inflationary pressure, the BoE may need to raise rates further. Bailey expects energy’s indirect effect to lift inflation by about 0.5 percentage point in H2 2026, but the pass-through is occurring more slowly than in the April forecast. Domestic inflation is broadly easing; household inflation expectations have eased but remain elevated. The latest CPI print was below the BoE’s expectation, a welcome sign that inflation is continuing to fall.

Brazil Unemployment Matches Forecasts at 5.4% — Brazil's unemployment rate fell to 5.4% in the rolling quarter ending in June 2026, down from 6.1% in the previous rolling quarter, matching market expectations and marking the lowest reading since January. The number of unemployed dropped 10.9% from the previous quarter to 5.9 million. Employment gains were concentrated in transportation, storage and postal services (3.9%) and public administration, education and health services (2.6%). The broader labor underutilization rate declined to 12.9% from 14.3%, while the number of underemployed workers fell 6.6% to 4.1 million. Total employment rose 1.1% to a record 103.1 million people, lifting the employment rate to 58.8% from 58.2%. Meanwhile, average real monthly earnings were unchanged from the previous quarter at BRL 3,738.

### 2026-07-30 20:19:09  #國際

【日本透過“H3火箭8號”發射失敗最終報告】日本文部科學省30日召開關於去年“H3火箭8號”發射失敗的會議。會議透過的最終報告指出，火箭搭載衛星的基座因內部剝離出現縫隙，縫隙在火箭飛行中擴大並導致基座破損是發射失敗的主要原因。 這份報告由日本宇宙航空研究開發機構整理成文。報告說，衛星整流罩分離開始後不久，搭載衛星的基座出現損傷。調查確定，基座破損的主要原因是，基座在生產過程中發生內部剝離，出現的縫隙在整流罩分離時的衝擊下進一步擴大。報告認為，今後可用樹脂填充或緊韌體連線等方式避免剝離風險。 日本2025年12月22日發射“H3火箭8號”，火箭第二級發動機燃燒提前停止，搭載的衛星未能進入預定軌道，發射任務失敗。(新華社)

Mexican GDP Expands Most Since Q1 2022 — The Mexican GDP rose 1.5% from the previous quarter in the second quarter of 2026, rebounding from a 0.6% contraction in the first quarter of the year. This marks the strongest growth since the first quarter of 2022, exceeding market's expectations of a 1.2% increase. The increase was in line with Bank of Mexico's outlook, that left its interest rate unchanged amid easing inflation over the quarter. Growth was driven by primary activities, which rose 3.3%, while secondary activities expanded 4.6% and the services sector grew 1.5%. Economic activity increased 1.2% from the previous year, marking the strongest yearly expansion since the fourth quarter of 2023, supported mainly by primary activities.

Bund Yield Holds Firm on Strong Eurozone Data and ECB Rate Bets — Germany's 10-year Bund yield held above 3.15% on Thursday as stronger-than-expected eurozone growth and firmer inflation reinforced expectations that the European Central Bank could deliver a second interest rate hike this year, potentially as soon as September. The eurozone economy expanded 0.4% in the second quarter, beating forecasts of 0.2% and marking its strongest growth since early 2025. Spain led with 0.7% growth, while Germany, France, and Italy each expanded 0.2%, and the Netherlands grew 0.4%. Higher inflation readings in Germany and Spain further supported the case for additional ECB tightening. Meanwhile, renewed US airstrikes on Iran lifted oil prices and dampened risk appetite, while the Federal Reserve added to policy uncertainty by keeping interest rates unchanged despite three FOMC members dissenting in favor of a rate hike.

### 2026-07-30 20:11:05  #其他

【工業和資訊化部量子資訊標準化技術委員會成立】7月30日，工業和資訊化部量子資訊標準化技術委員會成立大會在北京召開。第一屆量子資訊標委會由62名委員組成。 成立量子資訊標委會，是充分發揮標準的基礎性、戰略性、引領性作用，引導和推動量子產業高質量發展，加強量子領域國際合作的重要舉措。量子資訊標委會主要負責量子資訊基礎共性技術、量子計算、量子通訊、量子精密測量等領域行業標準制修訂工作，秘書處設在中國資訊通訊研究院。 成立大會後，量子資訊標委會第一次全體委員會議召開，審議透過了標委會章程、秘書處工作細則、工作組管理辦法、標準體系、標準制修訂工作程式等檔案，研究部署了下一階段重點工作。(新華社)

### 2026-07-30 20:09:48  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Currency #Importance 1

Euro Rises on Strong Eurozone Data and ECB Rate Hike Bets — The euro strengthened to $1.148, its strongest level since June 17, as stronger-than-expected eurozone economic data reinforced expectations that the European Central Bank could deliver a second interest rate hike this year, potentially as soon as September, offsetting concerns over rising Middle East tensions and a divided Federal Reserve. The eurozone economy expanded by 0.4% in the second quarter, beating forecasts of 0.2% and marking its strongest growth since early 2025. Spain led with a 0.7% expansion, while Germany, France, and Italy each grew 0.2%. Firmer inflation readings in Germany and Spain also supported expectations of further ECB tightening. Meanwhile, renewed US airstrikes on Iran lifted oil prices and weighed on risk sentiment. The Federal Reserve added to policy uncertainty on Wednesday by keeping interest rates unchanged, despite three FOMC members dissenting in favor of a rate hike.

【Germany July core inflation slows】Germany's July 2026 headline inflation is forecast at 2.8% YoY, with core inflation excluding food and energy seen at 2.4% YoY. Energy prices are projected to rise 8.3% YoY, with the pace accelerating sharply from the prior month. Analyst Giuseppe Dellamotta said core disinflation is positive for the ECB; if euro-area CPI follows a similar path, the likelihood of a September ECB rate hike could fall. Markets will watch developments in the Middle East — any de‑escalation would materially reduce the urgency for further ECB tightening.

### 2026-07-30 20:05:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Inflation Rate #Importance 3

German Inflation Accelerates to Three-Month High — Germany's annual inflation rate accelerated to 2.8% in July 2026 from 2.3% in June, exceeding market expectations of 2.7%, according to preliminary estimates. It was the highest reading since April, driven by a sharp pickup in energy inflation to 8.3% from 3.4% as renewed Middle East tensions pushed oil prices higher. Meanwhile, services inflation eased to 2.9% from 3.1%, while food inflation remained unchanged at 0.4%. Core inflation, which excludes energy and unprocessed food, edged down to 2.4% from 2.5%. The EU-harmonized inflation rate also rose to 2.8%, its highest level in three months and well above the European Central Bank's 2.0% target.

### 2026-07-30 19:52:50  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 0

Dollar Extends Losses — The dollar index fell below 100.8 on Thursday, extending the previous session's 0.6% decline to its lowest level in about two weeks, as investors continued to assess the latest FOMC decision and Fed Chair Warsh's press conference. As expected, the Fed left the federal funds rate unchanged, although three FOMC members voted in favor of a rate hike. While Chair Warsh reiterated the Fed's commitment to bringing inflation under control and stressed that policymakers would not hesitate to act if necessary, he was not among those advocating an immediate increase and refrained from providing clear forward guidance. As a result, investors increasingly viewed the central bank as merely postponing what they see as an inevitable rate hike. The implied probability of a 25bps increase in September has fallen to around 63%, down from nearly 80% before the Fed's decision. However, additional US strikes against Iran helped limit the dollar's losses by boosting demand for safe-haven assets.

He noted a 6-3 MPC split was narrower than expected and that inflation concerns currently outweigh growth worries, keeping a September rate hike live. US Futures Rebound on Thursday — Futures tracking US equities were firmly higher on Thursday, trimming losses from the previous session following a batch of corporate earnings. Contracts for the S&P 500 and the Dow were over 0.5% higher, while those for the Nasdaq 100 gained 1.5%. Microsoft surged nearly 10% premarket after posting a 43% growth in Azure revenue, the most since 2022 and ahead of expectations. This combined with capital expenditure forecasts that did not outpace expectations, limiting concerns of unsustainable AI spending. The result coincided with some respite for chip producers following their selloff yesterday, with Micron and Sandisk rising 3%. Conversely, Meta tanked 10% after its guidance missed expectations, while markets remained skeptical on whether its AI labs will drive revenue as signaled by the hyperscaler. Meanwhile, long-dated yields held their surge after the Fed Chair Warsh's reluctance to signal the Fed may raise rates triggered concerns that inflation could be higher for longer.

### 2026-07-30 19:48:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Loan Growth #Importance 1

Brazil Loan Growth Edges Up — The value of outstanding loans from the Brazilian financial sector rose by 0.7% from the previous month to R$7.4 trillion in June 2026. Loans extended to corporations rose 1.5% to R$2.7 trillion, while household lending edged up 0.2% to R$4.6 trillion. On an annual basis, total credit expanded 9.7%, slightly above the 9.6% growth recorded in May. Household lending continued to outpace corporate credit, rising 10.8% year-on-year, although easing from 11.2%, while lending to legal entities accelerated to 7.9% from 6.9%. Meanwhile, credit with free resources totaled R$4.2 trillion, up 0.7% from the previous month and 7.5% from a year earlier, as stronger corporate lending partly offset a monthly decline in household credit, which nevertheless remained higher than a year earlier.

### 2026-07-30 19:39:11  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #Market Regions #Europe & UK #GBPUSD #Impact bullish

【Bank of England says QT's impact on UK borrowing costs larger than expected】The Bank of England said its balance-sheet reduction has increased UK borrowing costs slightly more than previously estimated. In its Thursday monetary policy report the BoE said quantitative tightening lifted the 10-year gilt yield by about 20–30bps, versus a prior estimate of 15–25bps. The BoE will set the pace and size of balance-sheet reduction, including active gilt sales, in September for the coming year. While critics have blamed the plan for higher borrowing costs, the BoE said other factors, notably elevated inflation, were the main drivers. TD Securities strategist Pooja Kumra said the assessment signals the BoE is more accepting of QT’s effect on rates and reinforces market expectations that the pace of QT will slow further.

【截至6月底全國已建成投運新型儲能裝機規模達1.53億千瓦/3.96億千瓦時】記者30日從國家能源局舉行的新聞釋出會上獲悉，截至6月底，全國已建成投運新型儲能裝機規模達1.53億千瓦/3.96億千瓦時，同比增長61%。 新型儲能已成為促進新能源大規模開發消納、保障電力可靠供應和提高電力系統安全穩定水平的關鍵技術裝備。 釋出會上釋出的《中國新型儲能發展報告（2026）》顯示，“十四五”時期，新型儲能裝機規模快速發展，全國新型儲能裝機規模從“十四五”初期的300萬千瓦提升至1.36億千瓦，總體呈現逐年翻倍發展態勢。各類技術路線快速發展，逐步形成“鋰離子電池儲能主導，多技術路線並舉”的發展格局。 國家能源局能源節約和科技裝備司副司長徐繼林表示，“十四五”以來，我國新型儲能實現從小到大、從弱到強的跨越式發展，打造了新興產業發展的典型案例，為全球能源轉型貢獻“中國力量”。 “‘十五五’時期，國家能源局將積極推動新型儲能技術創新能力、產業發展水平穩居全球前列，以更大的力度、更務實的舉措推動新型儲能高質量發展。”徐繼林說。(新華社)

### 2026-07-30 19:30:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ukraine #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Ukraine Raises Interest Rate by 50bps — The National Bank of Ukraine raised its benchmark policy rate by 50bps to 15.5% in it July 2026 decision, its first rate hike in 16 months. The Board of the central bank stated that underlying inflationary pressures have risen in recent months and are likely to lift the headline inflation rate until the end of the year, warranting higher borrowing costs. The Board also noted that higher rates will increase the attractiveness of hryvnia-denominated assets and prevent higher levels of capital flight from households and businesses domestically. The NBU estimated that inflation could rise to the 10% level by the end of the year from the current 7.2% reading in June, before easing by next year. Meanwhile, expanded fiscal stimulus is expected to support economic growth, although inflationary responses are unlikely due to the impact of the Russian military attacks on aggregate demand.

### 2026-07-30 19:25:32  #央行 #市場

【英國央行以6比3票數維持利率不變 美伊戰爭陰雲令通脹前景難斷】英國央行將利率維持在3.75%不變。英國官員試圖在美伊緊張局勢再度升溫的風險與國內價格壓力降溫速度快於預期的跡象之間取得平衡。週四公佈的會議紀要顯示，貨幣政策委員會以六票贊成、三票反對的結果決定維持利率不變。首席經濟學家Huw Pill以及外部委員Megan Greene和Catherine Mann主張加息25個基點。在6月的會議上，僅Pill和Greene支援立即採取行動。英國央行維持政策指引，稱貨幣政策委員會“隨時準備採取行動”，以防高通脹持續，進而保留了政策選項。外部環境高度難以預測。就在決議公佈前幾天，油氣價格已明顯高於英國央行僅十天前在基準預測中採用的平均水平。不過，貨幣政策委員會表示，有“明確跡象”顯示國內通脹壓力正在緩解，迄今也“幾乎沒有證據”顯示能源衝擊已推高工資訴求，並帶動其他領域價格上漲。多數支援維持利率不變的委員還表示，如果戰爭很快結束，其戰略可能調整。其中兩名委員稱，在這種情況下會考慮降息。英國央行行長安德魯·貝利表示：“目前幾乎沒有出現第二輪效應的證據，儘管現在就因此感到寬慰仍為時過早。全球環境看起來更加不確定，也更易推高通脹，而國內形勢總體上在通脹前景方面更為良性，因此維持銀行利率不變是合適的。

【Franklin: Markets have already delivered part of Bank of England tightening; rate-hike bets rising】Franklin Templeton strategist Michael Browne said markets have effectively done much of the tightening for the 2026 MPC: elevated UK gilt yields and higher borrowing costs have tightened financial conditions without Bank of England intervention. He said that is unlikely to change as the Iran conflict lifts household energy bills, a new government’s fiscal plans remain uncertain and drought boosts food prices, keeping upside inflation risk. Markets are therefore likely to maintain tighter financial conditions. Investors now price at least one Bank of England hike before year-end and another by next spring, especially as expectations of further U.S. tightening build; Browne added the MPC is being pushed by shifting conditions rather than managing a delicate balance.

### 2026-07-30 19:21:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 2

US 10-Year Treasury Yield Continues to Rise — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note edged up to 4.69% on Thursday, extending the previous session's 7bps increase and returning to its highest level since January 2025. Meanwhile, the yield on the 30-year Treasury bond surged to 5.23%, its highest level in 19 years, as investors continued to assess the latest FOMC decision and Chair Warsh's press conference. As expected, the Fed left the federal funds rate unchanged but three FOMC members voted in favor of a rate hike. While Chair Warsh reiterated the Fed's commitment to bringing inflation under control and stressed that policymakers would not hesitate to act if necessary, he was not among those advocating an immediate increase and refrained from offering clear forward guidance, leaving investors viewing the central bank as merely postponing what they see as an inevitable rate hike. The implied probability of a 25-basis-point rate hike in September has fallen to around 63%, down from nearly 80% before the Fed's decision.

FTSE 100 Hovers at Record High After BoE Decision — The FTSE 100 traded higher on Thursday, hovering near a record high after the Bank of England voted 6-3 to keep its Bank Rate unchanged at 3.75%, compared with market expectations of a 7-2 split. Corporate earnings also drove market moves. Shell gained around 1.1% after reporting its strongest quarterly profit since early 2022 and announcing a fresh share buyback. Rolls-Royce surged more than 5% after upgrading its 2026 guidance, while BAE Systems advanced about 1% after raising its sales outlook. Lloyds Banking climbed 1% after beating profit forecasts, unveiling a new five-year strategy and launching a £1 billion share buyback. On the downside, LSE Group fell nearly 4% despite improving guidance and announcing a £1.4 billion buyback. British American Tobacco, Haleon, and Rentokil Initial also declined after earnings updates, with Rentokil plunging more than 16% on a cautious outlook despite meeting profit expectations.

### 2026-07-30 19:08:07  #MKT News #GBPUSD #Impact mixed #FTSE100 #DXY

【Bank of England holds Bank Rate at 3.75% by 6-3 vote as war risks cloud inflation outlook】The Bank of England held Bank Rate at 3.75% in a 6-3 vote (previous meeting 7-2). Greene, Mann and chief economist Pill voted for a hike. The committee retained guidance that it is prepared to act if necessary, citing renewed US‑Iran tensions and recent large energy-price swings. At the same time it said there are clear signs domestic inflationary pressures are easing and little evidence the energy shock has raised wage demands or materially passed through to other goods and services. A majority said policy could change if the war ends soon; two members said they would consider cuts in that scenario. Governor Bailey said there is currently little sign of second‑round effects but it is too early to be complacent given heightened global uncertainty and upside inflation risks, so holding rates is appropriate.

Indian Stocks Inch Higher — Indian stocks closed slightly higher on Thursday with support from upbeat earnings from auto manufacturers, offsetting the impact of higher energy prices and volatile bond markets. The S&P/BSE Sensex added 0.4% to 77,928 and the NSE Nifty 50 rose 0.3% to 24,317. Mahindra & Mahindra led the gains with a 2.2% jump after delivering strong results in the June quarter. Auto stocks followed to consolidate the sector's upswing, with Maruti Suzuki and Tata Motors both adding 1.6%. Pound Rises After BoE Holds Rates and Warns on Inflation Risks — Sterling strengthened to $1.34, its highest level since July 20, after the Bank of England voted 6-3 to keep the Bank Rate unchanged at 3.75%, with markets having expected a narrower 7-2 split. The BoE said the impact of higher energy prices remains uncertain and warned that inflation is likely to rise later this year as energy costs feed through to the economy. It added that the risk of persistent inflation has increased, with the outlook now tilted to the upside, although developments in the Middle East could still materially alter the outlook. Meanwhile, fresh US airstrikes on Iran in response to attacks on American forces across the Middle East lifted oil prices and weighed on risk sentiment. The Federal Reserve also added to uncertainty on Wednesday by leaving interest rates unchanged despite three FOMC members dissenting in favor of a rate hike.

### 2026-07-30 19:03:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Interest Rate #Importance 3

BoE Leaves Rates Unchanged, Flags Inflation Risks — The Bank of England left its Bank Rate unchanged at 3.75% at its July meeting, with a 6-3 majority in favor of holding rates steady, while three policymakers preferred a 25-basis-point increase to 4.0%. The decision reflects a balance between easing inflation and persistent risks from higher energy prices following recent tensions in the Middle East. Although consumer price inflation has slowed to 2.6%, the Bank expects it to rise again later this year as higher energy costs feed through to households and businesses. Encouragingly, there is little evidence so far of significant second-round inflation effects, while weaker labor market conditions and still-elevated borrowing costs are helping reduce underlying inflationary pressures. Nevertheless, the Committee believes inflation risks remain tilted to the upside due to geopolitical uncertainty and stands ready to tighten policy further if necessary to ensure inflation returns sustainably to its 2% target.

【IBM：已進入“量子優勢”時代】IBM週四表示，其研究顯示，量子計算機已經達到“量子優勢”階段——即量子計算機能夠執行超越傳統計算機能力的計算任務，並且這些結果可以透過嚴格方式進行驗證。這項研究成果標誌著這一新興技術向實際應用邁進一步，相關發現發表在IBM及其研究合作伙伴共同完成的三篇論文中。合作方包括芝加哥大學、日本理化學研究所以及量子計算軟體公司Qedma、BlueQubit和Algorithmiq。IBM研究部門負責人Jay Gambetta表示：“我們已經進入量子優勢時代。科學家們可以信賴這一成果。目前，重點已從單純對系統進行基準測試，轉向利用這些系統開展科學研究並探索實際應用。”

Ireland Harmonised Inflation at 5-Month Low — The EU-standard Harmonised Index of Consumer Prices in Ireland rose 3.1% year-on-year in July 2026, easing from a 3.2% increase in the previous month and marking the lowest reading since February, according to preliminary estimates. The slowdown was driven by softer energy inflation, which decelerated to 6.5% from 10.0% in June. Meanwhile, the HICP excluding energy and unprocessed food accelerated to 2.9% from 2.5%, food price inflation edged up to 0.8% from 0.5%, and services inflation ticked higher to 3.7% from 3.6%. On a monthly basis, harmonised consumer prices were unchanged in July, following a 0.1% increase in the previous month.

Portugal Retail Sales Up 3% YoY — Retail sales in Portugal increased 3% year-on-year in June 2026, following a downwardly revised 2.8% gain in May. Strong increases were recorded for household goods (7.5% vs 10.8%); textiles, clothing and footwear (8.1% vs 11%); computers, telecommunication equipment and books (4.2% vs 0.9%); pharmaceutical products and cosmetics (5.2% vs 5.7%) and for online and mail sales (17.1% vs 10.3%). ### 2026-07-30 18:16:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malta #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Malta Producer Inflation Steady at 1.5% in June — Producer prices in Malta increased 1.5% year-on-year in June 2026, matching the revised reading in the previous month and remaining at their highest level since October 2023 for a second consecutive month. Energy prices were unchanged from the previous month. Meanwhile, inflation eased for intermediate goods (2.7% vs. 2.9%) and capital goods (5.0% vs. 5.1%), while consumer goods prices were flat after declining 0.4% in the previous month. Within consumer goods, durable goods held steady at 0.1%, while non-durable goods were also unchanged after falling 0.5% in May. By market, producer inflation accelerated for both the domestic market (1.0% vs. 0.9%) and the non-domestic market (2.1% vs. 2.0%), although growth for exports to the Euro Area eased slightly (3.3% vs. 3.4%) while that for non-Euro Area markets picked up (1.0% vs. 0.8%). On a monthly basis, producer prices were unchanged in June, after rising 1.2% in the previous month.

### 2026-07-30 18:16:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Italy Producer Inflation Slows in June — Italy's producer prices increased 5.8% year-on-year in June 2026, easing from a 7.3% rise in the previous month. Domestic producer inflation slowed to 6.8% from 9.1%, while prices excluding energy accelerated slightly (2.9% vs 2.5%). Within manufacturing, price growth slowed for food, beverages and tobacco (-1.8%) and computer, electronic and optical products (-0.6%), while prices increased for coke and refined petroleum products (42.2%) and chemicals (8.0%). Meanwhile, inflation for electricity and gas supply eased to 8.9% from 12.6%. On foreign markets, producer prices accelerated to 3.1% from 2.7%, with prices rising 3.4% in the euro area and 2.8% outside it. Abroad, prices rose most for coke and refined petroleum products (18.1% non-euro area). On a monthly basis, producer prices were unchanged in June after a 0.2% decline in May.

### 2026-07-30 18:15:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Montenegro #Tourist Arrivals #Importance 1

Montenegro Tourist Arrivals Climb 5.8% in June — The number of tourist arrivals in Montenegro rose by 5.8% year-on-year to a ten-month high of 206,990 in June 2026, accelerating from 0.3% in the previous month. Foreign visitors remained the primary source of arrivals, accounting for 91.4% of the total, or 189,266 arrivals, while domestic arrivals represented 8.6%, or 17,724. Among foreign tourists, Europe continued to be the largest source region, contributing 78.8% of arrivals, led by Serbia, the United Kingdom, Bosnia and Herzegovina, and Poland. Outside Europe, the largest numbers of arrivals were recorded from Israel, the United States, and China (including Hong Kong). By type of accommodation, seaside resorts remained the most popular area, accounting for 81.9% of arrivals, followed by the capital region (10.3%) and mountain resorts (4.7%). Meanwhile, overnight stays in collective accommodation totaled 793,956, with foreign visitors accounting for 90.8% of stays and domestic tourists accounting for the remaining 9.2%.

### 2026-07-30 17:58:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Italy Jobless Rate at 7-Month High — The unemployment rate in Italy rose to 5.7% in June 2026 from an upwardly revised 5.3% in May, exceeding market expectations of 5.1%. This marked the highest level since November 2025, as the number of unemployed individuals increased by 97 thousand from the previous month to a seven-month high of 1.46 million. Meanwhile, the employment rate remained unchanged at 62.9%, with the number of employed persons edging higher by 1 thousand to 24.31 million. Temporary employees increased by 91 thousand from the previous month, while permanent and self-employed workers declined by 46 thousand and 44 thousand, respectively. The youth unemployment rate, measuring jobseekers aged 15 to 24, advanced to 18.4% in June from a record low of 16.9% in May. The inactivity rate edged lower to 33.2% from 33.5%.

【甘肅將重大氣象保障（暴雨）Ⅳ級應急響應升級為Ⅲ級】甘肅省氣象局於30日15時30分將重大氣象保障（暴雨）Ⅳ級應急響應升級為Ⅲ級。記者從甘肅省氣象局獲悉，30日8時至14時，甘肅省河西中部、河東部分地方出現降水，其中隴南市東南部、平涼市中部出現暴雨。隴南市、平涼市、臨夏回族自治州、慶陽市共出現15站次1小時降水量大於20毫米的短時強降水，最大小時雨強41.8毫米。甘肅省氣象臺預計，7月30日至8月1日隴南市東部和南部、天水市東南部、平涼市中部有大暴雨，最大累積雨量140至180毫米，區域性地方可達200毫米以上。甘肅省氣象局提示，此輪強降水具有對流性強、小時雨強大、持續時間長、累積雨量大等特點，且與前期降水落區有疊加，引發中小河流洪水、山洪、地質災害及城鄉積澇等次生災害的風險高，各地需要注意防範。（新華社）

### 2026-07-30 17:49:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #Belgium #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Belgium Annual Inflation Edges Up in July — Belgium's annual inflation rate edged up to 3.56% in July 2026 from 3.4% in the previous month. Energy inflation accelerated slightly to 10.6% from 10.3%, while electricity prices rose at a faster pace (7.9% vs 6.2%). Additional upward pressure came from housing and utilities (4.6% vs 3.6%), driven in part by faster rent inflation (3.4% vs 3.2%), as well as recreation and culture (8.3% vs 8.1%), health (6.7% vs 6.6%), and insurance and financial services (2% vs 1.8%). Meanwhile, inflation eased for transport (5.5% vs 7.9%), restaurants and hotels (4.1% vs 5.9%), clothing and footwear (1.2% vs 2.3%), furnishings (0.1% vs 0.4%), and information and communication (1.6% vs 1.9%). Food prices fell 0.12% after rising 1.2% in June. On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased 0.63%, up from a 0.3% rise in the previous month.

### 2026-07-30 17:49:30  #其他

【國際貨幣基金組織肯定澳門經濟韌性】國際貨幣基金組織（IMF）29日指出，澳門經濟保持韌性，金融體系整體穩健，金融業態日益多元，並在經濟適度多元發展方面取得穩步進展。 這是記者從澳門金融管理局獲悉的。據介紹，IMF專家代表團7月14日至23日訪澳，對澳門特區進行第四條款磋商，並就澳門的宏觀經濟及金融狀況作出初步總結。 第四條款磋商是IMF每年對成員經濟表現和宏觀政策的例行判斷與評估。IMF專家指出，在全球不確定性加劇的背景下，澳門經濟仍保持韌性，預期澳門經濟今年將錄得3.3%的增長，中期增長率則大致穩定在3.0%左右。同時，IMF專家對特區政府的財政實力給予正面評價，並經評估認為澳門金融體系整體保持穩健，金融業態呈日益多元化的發展趨勢。 IMF專家肯定澳門在經濟適度多元發展方面取得的穩步進展，並指出橫琴粵澳深度合作區的產能優勢以及澳門與葡語國家之間的緊密聯絡，將可為澳門創造新的發展機遇。此外，IMF專家認為特區政府設立的引導基金具催化作用，將有助支援澳門企業實現多元化發展。 特區政府表示，感謝IMF代表團的來訪與專業評估，將持續鞏固財政金融穩健基礎，促進經濟適度多元及高質量發展。(新華社)

### 2026-07-30 17:45:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

South Africa Producer Inflation Eases in June — Producer price inflation in South Africa rose by 7.5% in June 2026, easing from an over three-year high of 7.8% in the previous month. The slowdown was driven largely by decreases recorded in food, beverages, and tobacco (1.3% vs 2.1% in May), paper and printed products (8.5% vs 8.7%), electrical machinery, and communications and metering equipment (6.2% vs 6.9%), transport equipment (0.6% vs 0.7%), and furniture (4.6% vs 8.2%). On the other hand, costs increased for textiles, clothing, and footwear (6.9% vs 5.2%), non-metallic mineral products (11.5% vs 10.5%), and metals, machinery, equipment, and computing equipment (3.2% vs 2.9%), while inflation remained steady for coke, petroleum, chemical, rubber, and plastic products (at 22.0%). On a monthly basis, producer costs fell by 0.1%, following a 2.6% growth in the preceding period.

### 2026-07-30 17:41:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 1

Portugal's Economy Rebounds Strongly in Q2 — Portugal's economy expanded by 0.8% in the second quarter of 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 0.1% growth in the previous quarter, according to preliminary estimates. The rebound was driven by a positive contribution from net exports and stronger private consumption, although investment declined. The recovery followed a weak first quarter, when severe storms disrupted activity in central Portugal, while a surge in energy prices linked to the conflict in Iran weighed on growth. On an annual basis, GDP growth edged up to 2.5% from a revised 2.4% in the previous quarter. The government expects the economy to expand by 2.0% this year, while the Bank of Portugal projects slightly slower growth of 1.8%, following 1.9% growth in 2025.

Eurozone Economic Sentiment Improves More Than Expected — The Economic Sentiment Indicator for the Eurozone rose to 96.9 in July of 2026 from the upwardly revised 95.4 in the previous month, ahead of the median market consensus of 96. It was the highest reading since February, reflecting an improvement in sentiment from the momentary drop in energy prices and borrowing costs on the short-lived truce in the Middle East war. The confidence indicator for services providers improved to 4.7 from the upwardly revised 4.2. Meanwhile, industrial pessimism softened to -6.1 from 7.5. Among the Euro Area's largest economies, the ESI rose for Germany (97.2 vs 91.6), France (95.9 vs 93.5), Italy (99.6 vs 99.2), and Spain (104.3 vs 103.5).

### 2026-07-30 17:28:38  #MKT News #Nikkei 225 #Impact bullish #USDJPY #XAUUSD #Impact bearish

【Takaichi proposes cut in Japan food consumption tax to 1% for two years】Japan PM Takaichi announced a plan to cut the food consumption tax from 8% to 1% from April next year for two years and to issue cash transfers to low- and middle-income households equal to the 1% tax amount. Cabinet approval is expected in early August. The measure would cost roughly 5 tln yen a year, leaving questions over financing and political pressure when the rate reverts after two years. Nikkei-cited private analysis estimates FY2027 CPI could be 1.0–1.4 percentage points lower if the cut is implemented. Market commentary notes firms may absorb much of the cut rather than fully pass it into lower prices, reducing consumer gain, and warns of a potential one-off price rebound when the tax returns to 8%.

### 2026-07-30 17:26:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Montenegro #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Montenegro Trade Deficit Hits Record High — Montenegro's trade deficit widened to €396.5 million in June 2026 from €366.4 million a year earlier. This marks the highest trade deficit on record, as imports rose 7.7%, outpacing a 3.2% increase in exports. In the first half of the year, imports increased 3.4% year-on-year to €2,177.9 million, driven mainly by higher purchases of mineral fuels, lubricants and related materials (12.3%), animal oils and fats (8.4%), chemicals (5.9%), and machinery and transport equipment (1.6%). Meanwhile, exports fell 7.4% to €261.4 million, weighed down by lower shipments of machinery and transport equipment (-39.1%), chemicals (-27.6%), and crude materials (-18.4%). Europe remained Montenegro's main trading partner, accounting for 94.3% of exports and 78.8% of imports.

Eurozone Annual Growth Beats Expectations in Q2 — The eurozone economy grew 1.0% year-on-year in the second quarter of 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 0.5% in the previous quarter and well above market expectations of 0.5%, according to preliminary estimates.
